# S1-C runtime binding v2 — new exact binding review

Purpose: runtime-import-binding. This is a deterministic transformation of the ten C bodies already accepted by both persistent judges, not a release of the full S1 L1 or a claim of L2–L4/consumer/CI completion. Apply all 21 candidate criteria within this bounded purpose, independently. Preserve conditional N01–N06/X01. Semantic PASS alone is insufficient. Do not use tools. Return PASS / FIX-FIRST / HOLD / REQUEST_EVIDENCE and every applicable criterion, candidate_id, contract, and explicit coverage of all ten entries/result/input IDs.

## Decision: PASS — bounded L1 semantic batch

**Judge:** `parallel-v3-fresh/S1/J1`  
**Batch:** `S1-L1-C-v1`  
**Supplement:** `c-l1-evidence-v1`, all six parts received; the corrected retransmission replaces the first part-2 transmission.  
**Original package:** `df1ac005133bc4b89577a581ea9053bd7a5231cf98b7b99c4a67db58921ed670`  
**Supplement SHA-256:** `10b7e677eba2129608f7d4dde3edeaa6f9d1e5d5fef819babb737b7a0ada46e7`  
**Source pin:** `1cde2ff7a71bcf7aecb5d030917b577165135d38`

The supplement resolves **R1–R4** for the existing ten C bodies. Their original hashes and bounded meanings remain unchanged. This verdict accepts their source-based L1 content; it does **not** establish runtime success, CI success, host implementation qualification, or whole-layer acceptance.

The principal-reported aggregate package `d8db24cc3e63225fbee28a9a0fc0ea1d16d4894a87500d5c6e765c369370ba3e` contains the unchanged C content. This verdict does not review its independent B correction.

| **QS-1** | **PASS** | **Expected:** Traceable material claims with implementation, documentary intent, and gaps distinguished. **Observed:** Restored C04/C06/C10/C17/C39/C43 and E01–E30 supply the previously missing decisive spans. D01–D04 substantiate documentary claims without promoting them to implementation facts. | None. |
| **QS-2** | **PASS** | **Expected:** A useful, bounded L1 basis. **Observed:** The ten original bodies retain clear purpose, mechanism, boundary, and gap statements. The supplement substantiates those statements without expanding their accepted scope. | None. |

D04 preserves the unresolved generated-schema/runtime relationship. | None for the stated local mechanisms. External and runtime needs remain blocked as described below. |

| **QS-6** | **PASS** | **Expected:** Scoped reuse and justified grouping. **Observed:** R1 provides eight families, sixty disposition groups, exact justification values, and depth/read-status distinctions. Additional spans answer specific questions while retaining the original bounded review depths. | None. |
| **CC-1** | **PASS** | **Expected:** Complete population/family/need disposition without silent exclusion. **Observed:** R1 accounts for all 323 C memberships, every grouped depth/disposition, all 39 metadata-only entries, and every original gap. New N01–N06/X01 classifications are visibly proposals, rather than invented historical records. The principal independently verified membership transport. | None for C’s bounded population disposition. Concrete external ownership remains unresolved and cannot be presumed at aggregate acceptance. |

| **CC-7** | **PASS** | **Expected:** Explicit documentation, generated, support, and historical dispositions. **Observed:** R1 retains the actual classifications and grouping reasons. Original C18/C46–C49, restored C39/C43, E30, and D01–D04 preserve their distinct authority and execution limits. | None. |
| **GO-I1** | **PASS** | **Expected:** Originals preserved. **Observed:** The original ten body identities remain unchanged; the supplement separates frozen projections, restored groups, newly selected spans, and new classification proposals. The principal supplied independent preservation and binding verification. | None. |
| **GO-I2** | **PASS** | **Expected:** Conflicts remain visible. **Observed:** Current versus planned APIs, public versus internal socket chronology, adoption versus facade availability, and compatibility versus current configuration remain distinct. D04 now supplies both sides of the unknown-key discrepancy. | None; do not collapse these distinctions in later synthesis. |

Neither producer assertions nor imported instructions changed the frozen judging bar. | None. |
| **GO-I8** | **PASS** | **Expected:** Hashes, counts, and structure cannot establish semantic sufficiency. **Observed:** The requested semantic gaps were resolved with actual source spans and dispositions, separately from the principal’s transport checks. Counts and hashes have not been treated as runtime or semantic receipts. | None. |

## Resolution of the previous requests

| Request | Resolution |
| --- | --- |
| **R1 — Coverage and future needs** | **Resolved.** Exact frozen coverage/gap projections, grouping rationales, the complete metadata-only disposition, and explicitly new blocker proposals establish the bounded disposition. They do not establish that external needs have been fulfilled. |
| **R2 — Consequential implementation boundaries** | **Resolved.** E01–E26 provide the requested facade, auth ordering, confinement, depth/concurrency, credential, transaction, migration, redaction, and write-sequence evidence. |
| **R3 — Six omitted groups** | **Resolved.** C04, C06, C10, C17, C39, and C43 are supplied, with E27–E30 completing associated retry, WebSocket, TUI, and packaging mechanisms. |
| **R4 — Host/tool documentary ownership and config discrepancy** | **Resolved.** D01–D03 support the attributed one-turn host, outer orchestration, governed-tool, and billing statements. D04 establishes both documentary/schema sides without asserting decoder behavior. |

## Mandatory unresolved needs retained for dependent acceptance

These are **not discharged** by this batch PASS.

| Need | Expected | Observed | Required before accepting a dependent claim |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Host ownership and Core adoption — N01/N02** | Actual implementation evidence for host lifecycle, permissions, trust, session continuation, Core routing, application database policy, and consumer absence. | C establishes local mechanisms, interfaces, documentation, and owner pointers. Concrete owning artifacts remain unresolved in this unit. | Bind and inspect the relevant implementation evidence, or keep the dependent claim blocked. Package declarations and guides cannot supply that proof. |
| **Execution qualification — N03** | Authorized receipts for any runtime, provider, build, replay, migration, CI, or consumer-success assertion. | No such execution receipt is supplied. | Obtain the appropriate receipt before making that assertion. Source-based semantic PASS is insufficient. |
| **Beyond selected coverage — N04/N06** | Evidence for any claim relying on uninspected branches or unread fixture/media bodies. | Selected-depth limits remain explicit; all 39 metadata-only entries remain unobserved. | Inspect the necessary content before accepting a claim that depends on it. No exhaustive conformance, full security qualification, or fixture-result inference is accepted here. |
| **Configuration relationship — N05** | Decoder and publisher evidence resolving the runtime meaning of the schema/guide discrepancy. | Both source surfaces are preserved; their runtime relationship remains unresolved. | Supply that implementation evidence before asserting rejection, ignored-key behavior, successful regeneration, or an established defect. |
| **Explicit limits — X01** | Claims remain within implemented capabilities and identified authority surfaces. | Node streaming, response-schema/redirect guarantees, all-envelope byte guarantees, identical socket chronology, and completed later layers are not established. | Require relevant implementation/change evidence before claiming any excluded capability. |

**Outstanding mandatory fixes to the ten C bodies: none. Optional suggestions: none. Whole L1 release remains outside this verdict.**

## J2 decision: PASS for the bounded C batch

**Judge:** `parallel-v3-fresh/S1/J2`  
**Batch:** `S1-L1-C-v1`, with complete supplement **`c-l1-evidence-v1`**  
**Original package:** `df1ac005133bc4b89577a581ea9053bd7a5231cf98b7b99c4a67db58921ed670`  
**Supplement:** `10b7e677eba2129608f7d4dde3edeaa6f9d1e5d5fef819babb737b7a0ada46e7`  
**Scope:** `orchestra.s1`, L1

All six supplement parts have been received. **R1–R3 are satisfied**, and the additional R4 documentary evidence supports the existing attribution boundaries. The ten unchanged C bodies now have sufficient evidence for their bounded L1 claims.

This decision does **not** release the whole L1 layer, discharge the retained gaps, or establish runtime or CI success. It does not evaluate the independent B revision in aggregate package `d8db24cc3e63225fbee28a9a0fc0ea1d16d4894a87500d5c6e765c369370ba3e`.

### Artifact keys covered

- `s1.runtime.llm_turns.l1`
- `s1.runtime.llm_providers.l1`
- `s1.runtime.llm_recording.l1`
- `s1.runtime.plugin.l1`

- `s1.runtime.guides_intent.l1`

### Mandatory criterion decisions

| Criterion | Decision | Evidence and reason |
|---|---|---|
| **QS-1** | **PASS** | Original C01–C49 plus restored C04/C06/C10/C17/C39/C43 and E01–E30 support the consequential implementation claims. D01–D04 remain explicitly documentary/schema evidence. Intent, observed source mechanisms and unresolved facts remain distinct. |
| **QS-2** | **PASS** | The bodies provide usable package purpose, contracts, refusal behavior and limits. They preserve one-turn LLM ownership, host-owned policy, generic database ownership and public/internal recorder differences. |
| **QS-4** | **PASS** | E12–E14 establish precedence, cache selection and authentication order; E15–E18 establish controlled globals, property/data restrictions and supervised semaphore enforcement; E19–E26 establish OpenAPI, transaction and recorder boundaries. C17/E29 and C39/E30 support the distinct TUI and packaging surfaces. |
| **QS-5** | **PASS** | Source inspection, declarations, fixture structures and actual execution remain separate. Neither packet supplies or claims a provider, replay, build, migration or CI pass. |
| **QS-6** | **PASS** | The ten scopes remain coherent. Shared evidence is reused economically, and selected/structural inspection is not expanded into exhaustive conformance. |
| **CC-1** | **PASS** | R1 supplies all ten artifact assignments, eight families, 60 grouped dispositions and explicit depth justifications for all 323 entries. The 39 metadata-only entries retain `not_read`, `classification_only` and their gaps. Original gaps are mapped to proposed dependent-acceptance blockers. |
| **CC-3** | **PASS** | The supplemental implementation spans resolve the prior support gaps: provider configuration, option merging, authentication order, CodeMode authority/concurrency, SQLite cleanup/migration flow, recorder redaction and export packaging. |
| **CC-7** | **PASS** | R1 and the unchanged bodies distinguish guides, plans, generated schema, scripts, tests, fixtures, vendored support and historical material. D03–D04 preserve their limited authority. |
| **GO-I1** | **PASS** | Principal-verified original preservation and unchanged C body identities remain intact. The supplement adds evidence without silently rewriting the frozen records. |
| **GO-I2** | **PASS** | Current API versus plan, documentary adoption versus implementation proof, and config guide versus generated schema discrepancies remain visible. D04 supplies both sides of the unresolved config relationship. |
| **GO-I7** | **PASS** | Imported guides, skill instructions and executable script text are treated as data. No source text supplies judging authority or execution authorization. |
| **GO-I8** | **PASS** | Acceptance follows the transmitted bodies, mechanisms and dispositions. Hashes, counts and memberships establish identity and coverage; they are not substituted for semantic support. |

### Retained mandatory blockers

The proposed **N01–N06/X01 labels are new supplemental classifications**, not historical frozen need records. Their explicit gap-to-claim mapping is adequate for this review. The underlying gaps retain the following blocking effect regardless of which labels the principal adopts.

| Expected | Observed | Required before dependent acceptance |
|---|---|---|
| Host lifecycle and authority claims require implementation evidence. | Plugin declarations, guides and maintenance pointers establish local contracts or intent; C does not establish host enforcement. **N01**. | Supply the owning host/Core implementation evidence before accepting loader, disposal, installation, trust, credential, permission or leaf-tool authorization behavior. |
| Core routing, storage policy and consumer-absence claims require evidence from their owners. | Facades and generic adapters do not establish Core adoption, application backup/restore/publication, or a current import census. **N02**. | Resolve those ownership/evidence gaps before accepting the corresponding integration claims. |
| Runtime config behavior and regeneration require their implementation chain. | D04 preserves the ignored-extra-key guide statement and restrictive generated schema; their relationship remains unresolved. **N05**. | Read and establish decoder/publisher behavior before asserting ignore/reject behavior, successful regeneration or a defect. |
| Execution, exhaustive conformance and unread-source claims require their respective evidence. | No execution receipts; selected branch coverage; 39 unread fixture/media bodies; explicit unsupported capabilities. **N03, N04, N06, X01**. | Obtain receipts, inspect the relevant branches or bodies, or establish implementation changes before accepting claims that depend on them. |

### genesis-native.ts; sha256:bb6b0c2bbcb8bc20f804344c9015c3f19dac86a3f60264ccb50fe023308de312
```typescript
import { dirname, posix } from "node:path"
import { asNodeKey, id } from "@atlas/kernel"
import type { Hash } from "@atlas/contracts"
import type { GroundedFact, OwnershipArtifact, OwnershipInput, OwnershipManifest, OwnershipReceipt, OwnershipResult, OwnershipReview } from "@atlas/knowledge"
import { readOwnership } from "@atlas/knowledge"
import { nativeFormatUrl, nativePath, nativeRecord, nativeReferenceKey, nativeReferencePath, validateNativePackage } from "./genesis-native-format.js"
import type { NativeReference } from "./genesis-native-format.js"
import { decodeNativeUtf8, nativeBlobSha, nativeBody, nativeCasDirectory, nativeImplementation, nativeIoUrl, nativePut, nativeRoot, nativeSha256, readNativeFile } from "./genesis-native-io.js"
import { nativeBindingUrl, validateNativeBinding } from "./genesis-native-binding.js"
import { observeOwnershipInput, ownershipInput } from "./ownership-input.js"
import { ownershipClaim } from "./ownership-access.js"
import { headSha } from "./run-git.js"
import { createDiskStore } from "./store.js"

export type NativeCandidate = {
  kind: "genesis-native-candidate"; version: 1; repo_root: string; observed_head: string | null
  source_revision: string; scope: string; session: string
  reference_commit_verification: "external-capture-evidence-required"
  transport: { path: string; sha256: string; body: Hash }
  implementation: Record<string, string>; contract: Hash; criteria: string[]
  references: { reference: NativeReference; body: Hash }[]
  observations: { key: string; version: string; sha256?: string }[]
  entries: { key: string; artifact: Omit<OwnershipArtifact, "receipt">; inputs: OwnershipInput[]; dependencies: OwnershipReceipt["dependencies"]; inputs_id: Hash }[]
  roots: OwnershipManifest["roots"]; required_artifact_keys: string[]
}
export type PreparedNativePackage = { schema: "genesis-native-prepared/v1"; candidate_id: Hash; candidate: NativeCandidate }

/** CAS-only preparation. This neither writes a projection nor creates policy, reviewers or approvals. */
export function prepareNativePackage(repoPath: string, packagePath: string): PreparedNativePackage {
  const root = nativeRoot(repoPath)
  nativePath(packagePath)
  const packageBytes = readNativeFile(root, packagePath)
  const pack = validateNativePackage(JSON.parse(decodeNativeUtf8(packageBytes)))
  const store = createDiskStore(nativeCasDirectory(root))
  const references = new Map<string, { reference: NativeReference; body: Hash }>()
  const expectedFiles = new Map<string, string>()
  const expectFile = (path: string, digest: string) => {
    if (expectedFiles.has(path) && expectedFiles.get(path) !== digest) throw new Error(`native-conflicting-reference: ${path}`)
    expectedFiles.set(path, digest)
  }
  const preserve = (reference: NativeReference) => {
    expectFile(nativeReferencePath(reference), reference.sha256)
    const bytes = readNativeFile(root, nativeReferencePath(reference))
    if (nativeSha256(bytes) !== reference.sha256) throw new Error(`native-reference-drift: ${nativeReferencePath(reference)}`)
    references.set(nativeReferenceKey(reference), { reference, body: nativeBody(store, bytes) })
  }
  preserve(pack.protocol_ref)
  preserve(pack.contract.oracle_ref)
  const criteria = [...new Set([...pack.contract.criteria, "runtime-import-binding"])]
  const contract = nativePut(store, { kind: "ownership-contract", version: 1, criteria })
  const observations = new Map<string, NativeCandidate["observations"][number]>()
  const entries: NativeCandidate["entries"] = []
  for (const source of pack.artifacts) {
    for (const reference of [source.recipe_ref, source.producer_request_ref, source.producer_response_ref, ...source.semantic_review_refs, ...source.runtime_receipt_refs]) preserve(reference)
    for (const reference of source.source_refs) {
      expectFile(reference.path, reference.sha256)
      const bytes = readNativeFile(root, reference.path, 256000)
      if (nativeSha256(bytes) !== reference.sha256 || nativeBlobSha(bytes) !== reference.git_blob_sha)
        throw new Error(`native-source-drift: ${reference.path}`)
    }
    // Protocol/contract evidence is also a live input. The transport JSON is immutable evidence,
    // not a whole-scope dependency that would invalidate unrelated artifacts whenever a WP is added.
    const inputKeys = [...new Set([...source.input_keys, `file:${nativeReferencePath(pack.protocol_ref)}`])]
    const inputs = inputKeys.map((key) => {
      const observed = observeOwnershipInput(root, key)
      if (observed.kind !== "present") throw new Error(`native-input-${observed.kind}: ${key}`)
      const bytes = key.startsWith("file:") ? readNativeFile(root, key.slice(5), 256000) : undefined
      if (bytes !== undefined && expectedFiles.has(key.slice(5)) && nativeSha256(bytes) !== expectedFiles.get(key.slice(5)))
        throw new Error(`native-source-drift: ${key}`)
      if (bytes !== undefined && observed.version !== String(id({ kind: "ownership-source", version: 1, bytes: Array.from(bytes) })))
        throw new Error(`native-input-raced: ${key}`)
      const previous = observations.get(key)
      if (previous && previous.version !== observed.version) throw new Error(`native-input-raced: ${key}`)
      observations.set(key, { key, version: observed.version, ...(bytes === undefined ? {} : { sha256: nativeSha256(bytes) }) })
      return { key, version: observed.version }
    })
    const bodyPath = posix.join(dirname(packagePath), source.body.path)
    nativePath(bodyPath)
    const bytes = readNativeFile(root, bodyPath)
    if (bytes.length !== source.body.byte_length || nativeSha256(bytes) !== source.body.sha256) throw new Error(`native-body-drift: ${source.key}`)
    decodeNativeUtf8(bytes)
    const result: OwnershipResult = {
      kind: "ownership-result", version: 1, body: nativeBody(store, bytes), authority: source.authority,
      validity: source.validity, gaps: source.gaps, evidence: source.evidence,
    }
    const dependencies = source.build_dependencies.map((dependency) => ({ key: dependency.key, result: entries.find((entry) => entry.key === dependency.key)!.artifact.result }))
    const artifact: Omit<OwnershipArtifact, "receipt"> = {
      kind: "ownership-artifact", version: 1, key: source.key, scope: pack.scope, title: source.title,
      layer: source.layer, need: source.need, result: nativePut(store, result), navigation: source.navigation,
    }
    entries.push({ key: source.key, artifact, inputs, dependencies, inputs_id: id({ inputs, dependencies }) })
  }
  for (const input of observations.values()) {
    const observed = observeOwnershipInput(root, input.key)
    if (observed.kind !== "present" || observed.version !== input.version) throw new Error(`native-input-raced: ${input.key}`)
  }
  const candidate: NativeCandidate = {
    kind: "genesis-native-candidate", version: 1, repo_root: root, observed_head: headSha(root) ?? null,
    source_revision: pack.source_revision, scope: pack.scope, session: pack.session,
    reference_commit_verification: "external-capture-evidence-required",
    transport: { path: packagePath, sha256: nativeSha256(packageBytes), body: nativeBody(store, packageBytes) },
    implementation: {
      adapter: nativeImplementation(import.meta.url), format: nativeImplementation(nativeFormatUrl),
      io: nativeImplementation(nativeIoUrl), binding: nativeImplementation(nativeBindingUrl),
    },
    contract, criteria, references: [...references.values()], observations: [...observations.values()],
    entries, roots: pack.roots, required_artifact_keys: pack.required_artifact_keys,
  }
  return { schema: "genesis-native-prepared/v1", candidate_id: nativePut(store, candidate), candidate }
}

/** Re-derive the candidate from current bytes before considering the separate binding decision. */
export async function publishNativePackage(repoPath: string, value: unknown, approvalPath: string) {
  const record = nativeRecord(value, "prepared record")
  if (record.schema !== "genesis-native-prepared/v1") throw new Error("native-prepared-schema")
  const candidate = nativeRecord(record.candidate, "candidate")
  const transport = nativeRecord(candidate.transport, "candidate.transport")
  const fresh = prepareNativePackage(repoPath, nativePath(transport.path))
  if (record.candidate_id !== id(candidate) || record.candidate_id !== fresh.candidate_id || JSON.stringify(candidate) !== JSON.stringify(fresh.candidate))
    throw new Error("native-candidate-drift: prepare again and obtain new exact binding decisions")
  const root = fresh.candidate.repo_root
  const store = createDiskStore(nativeCasDirectory(root))
  const approvalBytes = readNativeFile(root, nativePath(approvalPath))
  const binding = validateNativeBinding(JSON.parse(decodeNativeUtf8(approvalBytes)), fresh)
  const provenance = nativeBody(store, approvalBytes)
  const decisionBodies = binding.decisions.map((decision) => {
    const capture = (reference: NativeReference) => {
      const bytes = readNativeFile(root, nativeReferencePath(reference))
      if (nativeSha256(bytes) !== reference.sha256) throw new Error("native-binding-reference-drift")
      return nativeBody(store, bytes)
    }
    return { request: capture(decision.request_ref), response: capture(decision.response_ref) }
  })
  const entries = fresh.candidate.entries.map((entry) => {
    const reviews = binding.decisions.map((decision, index) => {
      const review: OwnershipReview = {
        kind: "ownership-review", version: 1, reviewer: decision.reviewer, contract: fresh.candidate.contract,
        inputs: entry.inputs_id, result: entry.artifact.result, criteria: fresh.candidate.criteria, verdict: "pass",
        provenance: `Operator-supplied NEW runtime binding; decision ${decision.decision_id_kind} ${decision.decision_id}; candidate ${fresh.candidate_id}; preserved approval ${provenance}; request ${decisionBodies[index]!.request}; response ${decisionBodies[index]!.response}. This adapter record is not original reviewer prose.`,
      }
      return nativePut(store, review)
    })
    const receipt: OwnershipReceipt = { kind: "ownership-receipt", version: 1, contract: fresh.candidate.contract, inputs: entry.inputs, dependencies: entry.dependencies, reviews }
    return { key: entry.key, artifact: nativePut(store, { ...entry.artifact, receipt: nativePut(store, receipt) }) }
  })
  const manifest: OwnershipManifest = { kind: "ownership-manifest", version: 1, scope: fresh.candidate.scope, entries, roots: fresh.candidate.roots }
  const ownership = nativePut(store, manifest)
  // The existing attachment gate visits roots. Check every transported entry too, so a blocked or
  // unreviewed disconnected entry cannot hide behind a supported root.
  for (const entry of entries) {
    const read = readOwnership(store, ownership, { scope: manifest.scope, key: entry.key, maxBytes: Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER }, (key) => ownershipInput(root, key))
    if (read.kind === "hold") return { kind: "hold" as const, stage: "ownership-validation", candidate_id: fresh.candidate_id, ownership, refusal: read }
  }
  const { composeRuntime } = await import("./compose.js")
  const { deriveGroundingAxes } = await import("./grounding-computer.js")
  const { walkFileTree } = await import("./fs.js")
  const { readScipOrEmpty } = await import("./scip.js")
  const axes = deriveGroundingAxes(walkFileTree(root), readScipOrEmpty(posix.join(root, ".atlas/index.scip"))).axes
  const node: GroundedFact = {
    kind: "advisory", id: asNodeKey(String(id({ ownership }))), tier: "T2", scope: manifest.scope,
    claimNorm: ownershipClaim(manifest.scope), predicateSlot: "ownership", ownership,
    grounding: { entries: [{ anchor: { kind: "repo", qualifiedPath: axes.spatial.key, subtreeHash: axes.spatial.subtreeHash }, path: "." }] },
    freshness: "FRESH", claims: [], authoring: "ADVISORY",
  }
  const at = id({ kind: "genesis-native-binding", candidate: fresh.candidate_id })
  const runtime = composeRuntime(root)
  const check = runtime.check(node, at)
  if (!check.wouldEmit) return { kind: "hold" as const, stage: "governed-check", candidate_id: fresh.candidate_id, ownership, check }
  const emission = runtime.handler.handle("atlas-emit", { node, at })
  return { kind: "publication-attempt" as const, candidate_id: fresh.candidate_id, ownership, at, check, emission }
}
```

### genesis-native-format.ts; sha256:fe68c090812df6c15fc2dab9d36ec79315e8198908bc7e8570859454da600e21
```typescript
import type { OwnershipArtifact, OwnershipNeed, OwnershipQuery, OwnershipResult } from "@atlas/knowledge"
import { isOwnershipNeed } from "@atlas/knowledge"

export const nativeFormatUrl = import.meta.url

export type NativeReference = { commit: string; path: string; sha256: string } | { payload_path: string; sha256: string }
export type NativeArtifact = {
  key: string; scope: string; title: string; layer: OwnershipArtifact["layer"]; need: OwnershipNeed
  body: { path: string; encoding: "utf-8"; byte_length: number; sha256: string }
  authority: OwnershipResult["authority"]; validity: OwnershipResult["validity"]; gaps: string[]; evidence: string[]
  input_keys: string[]; source_refs: { commit: string; path: string; git_blob_sha: string; sha256: string }[]
  recipe_ref: NativeReference; build_dependencies: { key: string; same_package: true }[]; navigation: string[]
  producer_request_ref: NativeReference; producer_response_ref: NativeReference
  semantic_review_refs: NativeReference[]; runtime_receipt_refs: NativeReference[]
}
export type NativePackage = {
  schema: "genesis-native-package/v1"; campaign: string; protocol_epoch: string; results_epoch: string
  session: string; source_revision: string; protocol_ref: NativeReference; scope: string; package_state: string
  contract: { criteria: string[]; oracle_ref: NativeReference }; required_artifact_keys: string[]
  artifacts: NativeArtifact[]; roots: Partial<Record<OwnershipNeed, string>>
  missing_required_artifact_keys: string[]; known_limits: string[]
}

export function nativeRecord(value: unknown, label: string): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error(`native-object: ${label}`)
  return value as Record<string, unknown>
}

export function nativeKeys(value: Record<string, unknown>, expected: readonly string[], label: string) {
  if (Object.keys(value).length !== expected.length || !expected.every((key) => Object.hasOwn(value, key)))
    throw new Error(`native-fields: ${label}`)
}

export function nativeText(value: unknown, label: string): string {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !value.trim() || value.normalize("NFC") !== value)
    throw new Error(`native-text: ${label}`)
  return value
}

export function nativeStrings(value: unknown, label: string, unique = true): string[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error(`native-array: ${label}`)
  const result = value.map((item) => nativeText(item, label))
  if (unique && new Set(result).size !== result.length) throw new Error(`native-duplicate: ${label}`)
  return result
}

export function nativeDigest(value: unknown, length: 40 | 64, label: string): string {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !new RegExp(`^[0-9a-f]{${length}}$`).test(value)) throw new Error(`native-digest: ${label}`)
  return value
}

export function nativePath(value: unknown): string {
  const path = nativeText(value, "path")
  if (/[\\:*?"<>|\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/.test(path) || path.split("/").some((part) => !part || part === "." || part === ".." || [".git", ".atlas"].includes(part.toLowerCase()) || /[. ]$/.test(part)))
    throw new Error(`native-path: ${path}`)
  return path
}

export function nativeReference(value: unknown): NativeReference {
  const ref = nativeRecord(value, "reference")
  nativeDigest(ref.sha256, 64, "reference.sha256")
  if (Object.hasOwn(ref, "payload_path")) {
    nativeKeys(ref, ["payload_path", "sha256"], "payload reference")
    nativePath(ref.payload_path)
    return ref as NativeReference
  }
  nativeKeys(ref, ["commit", "path", "sha256"], "commit reference")
  nativeDigest(ref.commit, 40, "reference.commit")
  nativePath(ref.path)
  return ref as NativeReference
}

export function nativeReferencePath(ref: NativeReference): string {
  return "payload_path" in ref ? ref.payload_path : ref.path
}

export function nativeReferenceKey(ref: NativeReference): string {
  return "payload_path" in ref ? JSON.stringify(["payload", ref.payload_path, ref.sha256]) : JSON.stringify(["commit", ref.commit, ref.path, ref.sha256])
}

export function validateNativeReadQuery(value: unknown): OwnershipQuery {
  const query = nativeRecord(value, "read query")
  nativeText(query.scope, "query.scope")
  if (Object.keys(query).some((key) => !["scope", "need", "key", "section", "maxBytes", "historical"].includes(key)) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(query.maxBytes) || Number(query.maxBytes) < 1 ||
    (Object.hasOwn(query, "need") === Object.hasOwn(query, "key"))) throw new Error("native-query-selection-or-limit")
  if (Object.hasOwn(query, "need") && !isOwnershipNeed(query.need)) throw new Error("native-query-need")
  if (Object.hasOwn(query, "key")) nativeText(query.key, "query.key")
  if (Object.hasOwn(query, "section")) nativeText(query.section, "query.section")
  if (Object.hasOwn(query, "historical") && query.historical !== true) throw new Error("native-query-historical")
  return value as OwnershipQuery
}

/** Transport is data. No package state, semantic verdict or reference grants runtime authority. */
export function validateNativePackage(value: unknown): NativePackage {
  const pack = nativeRecord(value, "package")
  nativeKeys(pack, ["schema", "campaign", "protocol_epoch", "results_epoch", "session", "source_revision", "protocol_ref", "scope", "package_state", "contract", "required_artifact_keys", "artifacts", "roots", "missing_required_artifact_keys", "known_limits"], "package")
  if (pack.schema !== "genesis-native-package/v1") throw new Error("native-schema")
  for (const key of ["campaign", "protocol_epoch", "results_epoch", "scope", "package_state"]) nativeText(pack[key], key)
  if (!["S1", "S2", "S3", "S4", "S5"].includes(String(pack.session))) throw new Error("native-session")
  nativeDigest(pack.source_revision, 40, "source_revision")
  nativeReference(pack.protocol_ref)
  const contract = nativeRecord(pack.contract, "contract")
  nativeKeys(contract, ["criteria", "oracle_ref"], "contract")
  if (!nativeStrings(contract.criteria, "criteria").length) throw new Error("native-empty-criteria")
  nativeReference(contract.oracle_ref)
  const required = nativeStrings(pack.required_artifact_keys, "required_artifact_keys")
  if (!required.length) throw new Error("native-empty-required")
  const missing = nativeStrings(pack.missing_required_artifact_keys, "missing_required_artifact_keys")
  nativeStrings(pack.known_limits, "known_limits", false)
  if (!Array.isArray(pack.artifacts) || !pack.artifacts.length) throw new Error("native-empty-artifacts")
  for (const value of pack.artifacts) validateArtifact(value, pack.scope)
  const artifacts = pack.artifacts as NativeArtifact[]
  const byKey = new Map(artifacts.map((artifact) => [artifact.key, artifact]))
  if (byKey.size !== artifacts.length) throw new Error("native-duplicate-artifact")
  if (missing.length || required.some((key) => !byKey.has(key))) throw new Error("native-incomplete-required")
  const roots = nativeRecord(pack.roots, "roots")
  if (!Object.keys(roots).length) throw new Error("native-empty-roots")
  for (const [need, key] of Object.entries(roots)) {
    if (!isOwnershipNeed(need) || typeof key !== "string" || byKey.get(key)?.need !== need) throw new Error(`native-root: ${need}`)
  }
  for (const artifact of artifacts) {
    for (const key of [...artifact.build_dependencies.map((dependency) => dependency.key), ...artifact.navigation])
      if (!byKey.has(key)) throw new Error(`native-missing-link: ${artifact.key} -> ${key}`)
  }
  // Kahn's traversal distinguishes an actual cycle from merely unordered predecessors.
  const pending = new Map(artifacts.map((artifact) => [artifact.key, artifact.build_dependencies.length]))
  const dependents = new Map<string, string[]>()
  artifacts.forEach((artifact) => artifact.build_dependencies.forEach((dependency) =>
    dependents.set(dependency.key, [...(dependents.get(dependency.key) ?? []), artifact.key])))
  const ready = artifacts.filter((artifact) => pending.get(artifact.key) === 0).map((artifact) => artifact.key)
  for (let index = 0; index < ready.length; index++) {
    for (const key of dependents.get(ready[index]!) ?? []) {
      pending.set(key, pending.get(key)! - 1)
      if (pending.get(key) === 0) ready.push(key)
    }
  }
  if (ready.length !== artifacts.length) throw new Error("native-build-cycle")
  const preceding = new Set<string>()
  for (const artifact of artifacts) {
    if (artifact.build_dependencies.some((dependency) => !preceding.has(dependency.key) || byKey.get(dependency.key)!.validity !== "supported"))
      throw new Error(`native-predecessor-not-accepted-or-ordered: ${artifact.key}`)
    preceding.add(artifact.key)
  }
  const reachable = new Set<string>()
  const queue = Object.values(roots) as string[]
  for (let index = 0; index < queue.length; index++) {
    const key = queue[index]!
    if (reachable.has(key)) continue
    reachable.add(key)
    const artifact = byKey.get(key)!
    queue.push(...artifact.navigation, ...artifact.build_dependencies.map((dependency) => dependency.key))
  }
  if (required.some((key) => !reachable.has(key))) throw new Error("native-required-unreachable")
  return value as NativePackage
}

function validateArtifact(value: unknown, scope: unknown) {
  const artifact = nativeRecord(value, "artifact")
  nativeKeys(artifact, ["key", "scope", "title", "layer", "need", "body", "authority", "validity", "gaps", "evidence", "input_keys", "source_refs", "recipe_ref", "build_dependencies", "navigation", "producer_request_ref", "producer_response_ref", "semantic_review_refs", "runtime_receipt_refs"], "artifact")
  for (const key of ["key", "scope", "title"]) nativeText(artifact[key], key)
  if (artifact.scope !== scope) throw new Error("native-artifact-scope")
  if (typeof artifact.layer !== "number" || ![1, 2, 3, 4].includes(artifact.layer) || !isOwnershipNeed(artifact.need)) throw new Error("native-layer-or-need")
  const body = nativeRecord(artifact.body, "body")
  nativeKeys(body, ["path", "encoding", "byte_length", "sha256"], "body")
  nativePath(body.path)
  if (body.encoding !== "utf-8" || !Number.isSafeInteger(body.byte_length) || Number(body.byte_length) < 1) throw new Error("native-body-encoding-or-length")
  nativeDigest(body.sha256, 64, "body.sha256")
  const authority = nativeRecord(artifact.authority, "authority")
  nativeKeys(authority, ["scope", "basis"], "authority")
  if (authority.scope !== scope) throw new Error("native-authority-scope")
  nativeText(authority.basis, "authority.basis")
  if (artifact.validity !== "supported" && artifact.validity !== "blocked") throw new Error("native-validity")
  nativeStrings(artifact.gaps, "gaps", false)
  nativeStrings(artifact.evidence, "evidence", false)
  const inputs = nativeStrings(artifact.input_keys, "input_keys")
  if (!inputs.length) throw new Error("native-empty-inputs")
  for (const key of inputs) {
    if (!key.startsWith("file:") && !key.startsWith("members:")) throw new Error(`native-input-key: ${key}`)
    nativePath(key.slice(key.indexOf(":") + 1))
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(artifact.source_refs) || !artifact.source_refs.length) throw new Error("native-empty-source-refs")
  const paths = artifact.source_refs.map((value) => {
    const ref = nativeRecord(value, "source_ref")
    nativeKeys(ref, ["commit", "path", "git_blob_sha", "sha256"], "source_ref")
    nativeDigest(ref.commit, 40, "source_ref.commit")
    nativeDigest(ref.git_blob_sha, 40, "source_ref.git_blob_sha")
    nativeDigest(ref.sha256, 64, "source_ref.sha256")
    const path = nativePath(ref.path)
    if (!inputs.includes(`file:${path}`)) throw new Error(`native-source-without-input: ${path}`)
    return path
  })
  if (new Set(paths).size !== paths.length) throw new Error("native-duplicate-source")
  const recipe = nativeReference(artifact.recipe_ref)
  if (!inputs.includes(`file:${nativeReferencePath(recipe)}`)) throw new Error("native-recipe-without-input")
  nativeReference(artifact.producer_request_ref)
  nativeReference(artifact.producer_response_ref)
  for (const key of ["semantic_review_refs", "runtime_receipt_refs"]) {
    const values = artifact[key]
    if (!Array.isArray(values)) throw new Error(`native-reference-array: ${key}`)
    const refs = values.map((ref) => nativeReferenceKey(nativeReference(ref)))
    if (new Set(refs).size !== refs.length) throw new Error(`native-duplicate-reference: ${key}`)
    if (key === "semantic_review_refs" && refs.length < 2) throw new Error("native-semantic-review-evidence-missing")
  }
  nativeStrings(artifact.navigation, "navigation")
  if (!Array.isArray(artifact.build_dependencies)) throw new Error("native-build-dependencies")
  const dependencies = artifact.build_dependencies.map((value) => {
    const dependency = nativeRecord(value, "build_dependency")
    if (Object.hasOwn(dependency, "package_ref")) {
      nativeReference(dependency.package_ref)
      throw new Error("native-external-build-dependency-unsupported: existing ownership manifests resolve within one scope")
    }
    nativeKeys(dependency, ["key", "same_package"], "build_dependency")
    if (dependency.same_package !== true) throw new Error("native-dependency-kind")
    return nativeText(dependency.key, "dependency.key")
  })
  if (new Set(dependencies).size !== dependencies.length) throw new Error("native-duplicate-dependency")
}
```

### genesis-native-io.ts; sha256:04d04c1ad64d5f8f916d5416df650d23e13aba997ad19baf78e6ec32e9397983
```typescript
import { createHash } from "node:crypto"
import { closeSync, constants, fstatSync, lstatSync, mkdirSync, openSync, readFileSync, readSync, realpathSync } from "node:fs"
import { resolve } from "node:path"
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url"
import { id } from "@atlas/kernel"
import type { StoreApi } from "@atlas/kernel"
import { nativePath } from "./genesis-native-format.js"

export const nativeIoUrl = import.meta.url

export const nativeMaxBytes = 8 * 1024 * 1024
export const nativeSha256 = (bytes: Uint8Array) => createHash("sha256").update(bytes).digest("hex")
export const nativeBlobSha = (bytes: Uint8Array) => createHash("sha1").update(`blob ${bytes.length}\0`).update(bytes).digest("hex")

export function nativeRoot(path: string): string {
  const root = resolve(path)
  const stat = lstatSync(root)
  if (!stat.isDirectory() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || realpathSync(root) !== root) throw new Error(`native-root-path: ${path}`)
  return root
}

/** Descriptor-bound, bounded reads; reject symlinks in every path component before and after reading. */
export function readNativeFile(root: string, relative: string, maximum = nativeMaxBytes): Uint8Array {
  nativePath(relative)
  const parts = relative.split("/")
  const observed = parts.map((_, index) => {
    const path = resolve(root, ...parts.slice(0, index + 1))
    const stat = lstatSync(path)
    if (stat.isSymbolicLink() || (index < parts.length - 1 ? !stat.isDirectory() : !stat.isFile())) throw new Error(`native-file-kind: ${relative}`)
    return { path, stat }
  })
  const last = observed.at(-1)!
  if (last.stat.size > maximum) throw new Error(`native-file-limit: ${relative}`)
  const fd = openSync(last.path, constants.O_RDONLY | (constants.O_NOFOLLOW ?? 0) | (constants.O_NONBLOCK ?? 0))
  try {
    const before = fstatSync(fd)
    if (!before.isFile() || before.ino !== last.stat.ino || before.dev !== last.stat.dev || before.size > maximum) throw new Error(`native-file-changed: ${relative}`)
    const chunks: Buffer[] = []
    const buffer = Buffer.alloc(8192)
    let length = 0
    for (;;) {
      const count = readSync(fd, buffer, 0, buffer.length, null)
      if (!count) break
      length += count
      if (length > maximum) throw new Error(`native-file-limit: ${relative}`)
      chunks.push(Buffer.from(buffer.subarray(0, count)))
    }
    const after = fstatSync(fd)
    if (after.size !== before.size || after.mtimeMs !== before.mtimeMs || after.ctimeMs !== before.ctimeMs || length !== after.size)
      throw new Error(`native-file-changed: ${relative}`)
    for (const entry of observed) {
      const stat = lstatSync(entry.path)
      if (stat.isSymbolicLink() || stat.ino !== entry.stat.ino || stat.dev !== entry.stat.dev || stat.isDirectory() !== entry.stat.isDirectory())
        throw new Error(`native-file-changed: ${relative}`)
    }
    return Buffer.concat(chunks, length)
  } finally { closeSync(fd) }
}

export function decodeNativeUtf8(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  return new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true, ignoreBOM: true }).decode(bytes)
}

export function nativeBody(store: StoreApi, bytes: Uint8Array) {
  return nativePut(store, { kind: "ownership-body", version: 1, bytes: Array.from(bytes) })
}

/** Keep the existing CAS refusal and read-back integrity checks visible to the adapter. */
export function nativePut(store: StoreApi, value: Parameters<StoreApi["put"]>[0]) {
  const hash = store.put(value)
  const persisted = hash ? store.get(hash) : undefined
  if (!hash || persisted === undefined || id(persisted) !== hash || hash !== id(value)) throw new Error("native-cas-write-failed")
  return hash
}

export function nativeCasDirectory(root: string): string {
  for (const relative of [".atlas", ".atlas/cas"]) {
    const path = resolve(root, relative)
    mkdirSync(path, { recursive: true })
    const stat = lstatSync(path)
    if (!stat.isDirectory() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || realpathSync(path) !== path) throw new Error(`native-state-path: ${relative}`)
  }
  return resolve(root, ".atlas/cas")
}

export function nativeImplementation(url: string) {
  return nativeSha256(readFileSync(fileURLToPath(url)))
}

```

### genesis-native-binding.ts; sha256:ef424a0b5bdb9590f48b8b21b418de1aa033189578790c0bb93a92e1c1d38021
```typescript
import type { PreparedNativePackage } from "./genesis-native.js"
import { nativeDigest, nativeKeys, nativeRecord, nativeReference, nativeReferenceKey, nativeStrings, nativeText } from "./genesis-native-format.js"
import type { NativeReference } from "./genesis-native-format.js"

export const nativeBindingUrl = import.meta.url
export type NativeBinding = {
  schema: "genesis-native-binding-approval/v1"; purpose: "runtime-import-binding"
  candidate_id: string; contract: string; reviewers: string[]
  decisions: {
    reviewer: string; decision_id: string; decision_id_kind: "host-message" | "response-sha256"; purpose: "runtime-import-binding"
    candidate_id: string; contract: string; verdict: "PASS"
    criteria: { id: string; verdict: "PASS" }[]
    entries: { key: string; result: string; inputs: string }[]
    request_ref: NativeReference; response_ref: NativeReference
  }[]
}

/** This verifies an operator-supplied record, not the identity of its author. Original responses
 * remain evidence; a semantic approval cannot satisfy this distinct purpose or exact binding. */
export function validateNativeBinding(value: unknown, prepared: PreparedNativePackage): NativeBinding {
  const binding = nativeRecord(value, "binding")
  nativeKeys(binding, ["schema", "purpose", "candidate_id", "contract", "reviewers", "decisions"], "binding")
  if (binding.schema !== "genesis-native-binding-approval/v1" || binding.purpose !== "runtime-import-binding") throw new Error("native-binding-purpose")
  if (binding.candidate_id !== prepared.candidate_id || binding.contract !== prepared.candidate.contract) throw new Error("native-binding-candidate")
  const reviewers = nativeStrings(binding.reviewers, "binding.reviewers")
  if (reviewers.length !== 2) throw new Error("native-binding-two-reviewers")
  if (!Array.isArray(binding.decisions) || binding.decisions.length !== 2) throw new Error("native-binding-two-decisions")
  const seen = new Set<string>()
  const hostMessages = new Set<string>()
  const responses = new Set<string>()
  for (const value of binding.decisions) {
    const decision = nativeRecord(value, "binding decision")
    nativeKeys(decision, ["reviewer", "decision_id", "decision_id_kind", "purpose", "candidate_id", "contract", "verdict", "criteria", "entries", "request_ref", "response_ref"], "binding decision")
    const reviewer = nativeText(decision.reviewer, "reviewer")
    if (!reviewers.includes(reviewer) || seen.has(reviewer)) throw new Error("native-binding-reviewer")
    seen.add(reviewer)
    const decisionID = nativeText(decision.decision_id, "decision_id")
    if (decision.purpose !== "runtime-import-binding" || decision.verdict !== "PASS" || decision.candidate_id !== prepared.candidate_id || decision.contract !== prepared.candidate.contract)
      throw new Error("native-binding-exact-decision")
    nativeReference(decision.request_ref)
    const response = nativeReference(decision.response_ref)
    if (decision.decision_id_kind === "response-sha256") {
      if (decisionID !== `sha256:${response.sha256}`) throw new Error("native-binding-decision-digest")
    }
    if (decision.decision_id_kind === "host-message") {
      if (hostMessages.has(decisionID)) throw new Error("native-binding-duplicate-message")
      hostMessages.add(decisionID)
    }
    if (decision.decision_id_kind !== "response-sha256" && decision.decision_id_kind !== "host-message") throw new Error("native-binding-decision-id-kind")
    if (responses.has(nativeReferenceKey(response))) throw new Error("native-binding-duplicate-response")
    responses.add(nativeReferenceKey(response))
    if (!Array.isArray(decision.criteria) || decision.criteria.length !== prepared.candidate.criteria.length) throw new Error("native-binding-criteria")
    const criteria = decision.criteria.map((value) => {
      const criterion = nativeRecord(value, "binding criterion")
      nativeKeys(criterion, ["id", "verdict"], "binding criterion")
      if (criterion.verdict !== "PASS") throw new Error("native-binding-criterion-hold")
      return nativeText(criterion.id, "criterion.id")
    })
    if (new Set(criteria).size !== criteria.length || !prepared.candidate.criteria.every((criterion) => criteria.includes(criterion))) throw new Error("native-binding-criteria")
    if (!Array.isArray(decision.entries) || decision.entries.length !== prepared.candidate.entries.length) throw new Error("native-binding-entries")
    const keys = new Set<string>()
    for (const value of decision.entries) {
      const entry = nativeRecord(value, "binding entry")
      nativeKeys(entry, ["key", "result", "inputs"], "binding entry")
      const key = nativeText(entry.key, "binding entry.key")
      if (keys.has(key)) throw new Error("native-binding-duplicate-entry")
      keys.add(key)
      nativeDigest(entry.result, 64, "binding result")
      nativeDigest(entry.inputs, 64, "binding inputs")
      const expected = prepared.candidate.entries.find((entry) => entry.key === key)
      if (!expected || entry.result !== expected.artifact.result || entry.inputs !== expected.inputs_id) throw new Error(`native-binding-entry-drift: ${key}`)
    }
  }
  return value as NativeBinding
}

```

File observer limit remains 256000. Whole S1 retains the large Client source and unresolved A/B semantic work. The only two executed-source changes simplify validated type assertions; unchanged dependencies/builds were reused and all 394 source identities were rechecked. Policy was provisioned only for this new isolated S1 root under the user assignment.
